				clear_provenance("rsi")
				clear_provenance("rdi")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(stos|scas|ins)/) {
				clear_provenance("rdi")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^outs/) {
				clear_provenance("rsi")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(enter|leave|push|pop)/) {
				clear_provenance("rsp")
				if (instruction ~ /^(enter|leave)/)
					clear_provenance("rbp")
			}
			if (instruction ~ /^(loop|loope|loopne)/)
				clear_provenance("rcx")
			if (instruction ~ /^(rsm|syscall|sysret|rep)/)
				clear_provenance("")
		}
		function normalize(address) {
			sub(/^0x/, "", address)
			sub(/^0+/, "", address)
			return address == "" ? "0" : address
		}
		{
			line[NR] = $0
			if ($0 ~ /\t((lock|bnd)[[:space:]]+)?(j[a-z]+|loop[a-z]*)[[:space:]]+(0x)?[0-9a-f]+/) {
				destination = $0
				sub(/^.*\t((lock|bnd)[[:space:]]+)?(j[a-z]+|loop[a-z]*)[[:space:]]+/, "", destination)
				sub(/[[:space:]].*$/, "", destination)
				branch_target[normalize(destination)] = 1
			}
		}
		END {
			for (number = 1; number <= NR; number++) {
				text = line[number]
				if (text ~ /^Disassembly of section /) {
					clear_provenance("")
					continue
				}
				if (text ~ /^[0-9a-f]+ <[^>]+>:/) {
					clear_provenance("")
					continue
				}
				if (text !~ /^[[:space:]]*[0-9a-f]+:/)
					continue
				address = text
				sub(/^[[:space:]]*/, "", address)
				sub(/:.*/, "", address)
				address = normalize(address)
				if (address in branch_target)
					clear_provenance("")
				instruction = text
				sub(/^.*\t/, "", instruction)
				gsub(/[[:space:]]+/, "", instruction)
				opcode = instruction
				sub(/^(lock|bnd)/, "", opcode)
				if (opcode ~ /^(j[a-z]+|loop[a-z]*|call|ret)/) {
					clear_provenance("")
					continue
				}
				if (opcode ~ /^mov[a-z]*\$0x[0-9a-f]+,%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					source = opcode
					sub(/^mov[a-z]*\$0x/, "", source)
					sub(/,.*/, "", source)
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					destination_width = width(destination)
					destination = canonical(destination)
					if (destination != "") {
						if (destination_width >= 32)
							value[destination] = source
						else
							value[destination] = "dynamic"
					}
				} else if (opcode ~ /^mov[a-z]*%[a-z0-9]+,%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					source = opcode
					sub(/^mov[a-z]*/, "", source)
					sub(/,.*/, "", source)
					source_width = width(source)
					source = canonical(source)
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					destination_width = width(destination)
					destination = canonical(destination)
					if (destination != "") {
						if (destination_width >= 32 && source_width >= 32 &&
						    source in value)
							value[destination] = value[source]
						else
							value[destination] = "dynamic"
					}
				} else if (opcode ~ /^(xchg|xadd)/) {
					source = opcode
					sub(/^[^%]*/, "%", source)
					sub(/,.*/, "", source)
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(source))
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
				} else if (opcode ~ /^mulx[a-z]*%[a-z0-9]+,%[a-z0-9]+,%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^[^,]*,/, "", destination)
					sub(/,.*/, "", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
				} else if (opcode ~ /^(rdfsbase|rdgsbase|rdpid|rdsspq)[a-z]*%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^[^%]*/, "%", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
				} else if (opcode ~ /^(pop|inc|dec|neg|not|bswap|set[a-z]+|rol|ror|rcl|rcr|sal|sar|shl|shr|rdrand|rdseed)[a-z]*%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^[^%]*/, "%", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
				} else if (opcode ~ /,%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					destination = canonical(destination)
					clear_provenance(destination)
				}
				clear_implicit_destinations(opcode)

				     opcode ~ /%((r|e)?cx|c[lh])/)
					clear_provenance("rcx")
				if (opcode ~ /wrmsr/) {
					if (!("rcx" in value))
						print "unknown"
					else
						print value["rcx"]
					clear_provenance("")
				}
			}

		function canonical(register_name, name) {
			name = register_name
			gsub(/^%/, "", name)
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(ax|al|ah)$/) return "rax"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(bx|bl|bh)$/) return "rbx"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(cx|cl|ch)$/) return "rcx"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(dx|dl|dh)$/) return "rdx"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(si|sil)$/) return "rsi"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(di|dil)$/) return "rdi"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(bp|bpl)$/) return "rbp"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(sp|spl)$/) return "rsp"
			if (name ~ /^r(8|9|1[0-5])(d|w|b)?$/) {
				sub(/[dwb]$/, "", name)
				return name
			}
			return ""
		}
		function width(register_name, name) {
			name = register_name
			gsub(/^%/, "", name)
			if (name ~ /^r(ax|bx|cx|dx|si|di|bp|sp)$/ ||
			    name ~ /^r(8|9|1[0-5])$/)
				return 64
			if (name ~ /^e(ax|bx|cx|dx|si|di|bp|sp)$/ ||
			    name ~ /^r(8|9|1[0-5])d$/)
				return 32
			if (name ~ /^(ax|bx|cx|dx|si|di|bp|sp)$/ ||
			    name ~ /^r(8|9|1[0-5])w$/)
				return 16
			return 8
		}
		function clear_provenance(register_name) {
			if (register_name == "") {
				for (name in value)
					delete value[name]
			} else {
				delete value[register_name]
			}
		}
		function is_rdpru(instruction) {
			while (instruction ~ /^(addr16|addr32|cs|ds|es|fs|gs|ss)/ ||
			       instruction ~ /^rex(\.[WRXB]+)?/) {
				sub(/^(addr16|addr32|cs|ds|es|fs|gs|ss)/, "", instruction)
				sub(/^rex(\.[WRXB]+)?/, "", instruction)
			}
			return instruction == "rdpru"
		}
		function clear_implicit_destinations(instruction, operands) {
			operands = instruction
			gsub(/^[a-z0-9]+/, "", operands)
			if (instruction ~ /^(mul|div|idiv)[a-z]*%/ ||
			    (instruction ~ /^imul[a-z]*%/ && operands !~ /,/)) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(cbw|cwde|cdqe)$/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(cwd|cdq|cqo)$/) {
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(cpuid|getsec)$/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rbx")
				clear_provenance("rcx")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(rdmsr|rdtsc|xgetbv)$/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(rdpmc|rdpkru)$/ || is_rdpru(instruction)) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^rdtscp$/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rcx")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^cmpxchg(8b|16b)/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^cmpxchg/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(lahf|xlat)/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(lods)/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rsi")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(movs|cmps)/) {
				clear_provenance("rsi")
				clear_provenance("rdi")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(stos|scas|ins)/) {
				clear_provenance("rdi")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^outs/) {
				clear_provenance("rsi")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(enter|leave|push|pop)/) {
				clear_provenance("rsp")
				if (instruction ~ /^(enter|leave)/)
					clear_provenance("rbp")
			}
			if (instruction ~ /^(loop|loope|loopne)/)
				clear_provenance("rcx")
			if (instruction ~ /^(rsm|syscall|sysret|rep)/)
				clear_provenance("")
		}
		function normalize(address) {
			sub(/^0x/, "", address)
			sub(/^0+/, "", address)
			return address == "" ? "0" : address
		}
		{
			line[NR] = $0
			if ($0 ~ /\t((lock|bnd)[[:space:]]+)?(j[a-z]+|loop[a-z]*)[[:space:]]+(0x)?[0-9a-f]+/) {
				destination = $0
				sub(/^.*\t((lock|bnd)[[:space:]]+)?(j[a-z]+|loop[a-z]*)[[:space:]]+/, "", destination)
				sub(/[[:space:]].*$/, "", destination)
				branch_target[normalize(destination)] = 1
			}
		}
		END {
			for (number = 1; number <= NR; number++) {
				text = line[number]
				if (text ~ /^Disassembly of section /) {
					clear_provenance("")
					continue
				}
				if (text ~ /^[0-9a-f]+ <[^>]+>:/) {
					clear_provenance("")
					continue
				}
				if (text !~ /^[[:space:]]*[0-9a-f]+:/)
					continue
				address = text
				sub(/^[[:space:]]*/, "", address)
				sub(/:.*/, "", address)
				address = normalize(address)
				if (address in branch_target)
					clear_provenance("")
				instruction = text
				sub(/^.*\t/, "", instruction)
				gsub(/[[:space:]]+/, "", instruction)
				opcode = instruction
				sub(/^(lock|bnd)/, "", opcode)
				if (opcode ~ /^(j[a-z]+|loop[a-z]*|call|ret)/) {
					clear_provenance("")
					continue
				}
				if (opcode ~ /^mov[a-z]*\$0x[0-9a-f]+,%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					source = opcode
					sub(/^mov[a-z]*\$0x/, "", source)
					sub(/,.*/, "", source)
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					destination_width = width(destination)
					destination = canonical(destination)
					if (destination != "") {
						if (destination_width >= 32)
							value[destination] = source
						else
							value[destination] = "dynamic"
					}
				} else if (opcode ~ /^mov[a-z]*%[a-z0-9]+,%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					source = opcode
					sub(/^mov[a-z]*/, "", source)
					sub(/,.*/, "", source)
					source_width = width(source)
					source = canonical(source)
					destination = opcode

					destination_width = width(destination)

							value[destination] = "dynamic"

					destination = opcode

					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
					destination = opcode

					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))

			while (instruction ~ /^(addr16|addr32|cs|ds|es|fs|gs|ss)/ ||
			       instruction ~ /^rex(\.[WRXB]+)?/) {
				sub(/^(addr16|addr32|cs|ds|es|fs|gs|ss)/, "", instruction)
				sub(/^rex(\.[WRXB]+)?/, "", instruction)
			}
			return instruction == "rdpru"
		}
		function clear_implicit_destinations(instruction, operands) {
			operands = instruction
			gsub(/^[a-z0-9]+/, "", operands)
			if (instruction ~ /^(mul|div|idiv)[a-z]*%/ ||
			    (instruction ~ /^imul[a-z]*%/ && operands !~ /,/)) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(cbw|cwde|cdqe)$/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(cwd|cdq|cqo)$/) {
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(cpuid|getsec)$/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rbx")
				clear_provenance("rcx")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(rdmsr|rdtsc|xgetbv)$/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(rdpmc|rdpkru)$/ || is_rdpru(instruction)) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^rdtscp$/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rcx")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^cmpxchg(8b|16b)/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^cmpxchg/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(lahf|xlat)/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(lods)/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rsi")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(movs|cmps)/) {
				clear_provenance("rsi")
				clear_provenance("rdi")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(stos|scas|ins)/) {
				clear_provenance("rdi")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^outs/) {
				clear_provenance("rsi")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(enter|leave|push|pop)/) {
				clear_provenance("rsp")
				if (instruction ~ /^(enter|leave)/)
					clear_provenance("rbp")
			}
			if (instruction ~ /^(loop|loope|loopne)/)
				clear_provenance("rcx")
			if (instruction ~ /^(rsm|syscall|sysret|rep)/)
				clear_provenance("")
		}
		function normalize(address) {
			sub(/^0x/, "", address)
			sub(/^0+/, "", address)
			return address == "" ? "0" : address
		}
		{
			line[NR] = $0
			if ($0 ~ /\t((lock|bnd)[[:space:]]+)?(j[a-z]+|loop[a-z]*)[[:space:]]+(0x)?[0-9a-f]+/) {
				destination = $0
				sub(/^.*\t((lock|bnd)[[:space:]]+)?(j[a-z]+|loop[a-z]*)[[:space:]]+/, "", destination)
				sub(/[[:space:]].*$/, "", destination)
				branch_target[normalize(destination)] = 1
			}
		}
		END {
			for (number = 1; number <= NR; number++) {
				text = line[number]
				if (text ~ /^Disassembly of section /) {
					clear_provenance("")
					continue
				}
				if (text ~ /^[0-9a-f]+ <[^>]+>:/) {
					clear_provenance("")
					continue
				}
				if (text !~ /^[[:space:]]*[0-9a-f]+:/)
					continue
				address = text
				sub(/^[[:space:]]*/, "", address)
				sub(/:.*/, "", address)
				address = normalize(address)
				if (address in branch_target)
					clear_provenance("")
				instruction = text
				sub(/^.*\t/, "", instruction)
				gsub(/[[:space:]]+/, "", instruction)
				opcode = instruction
				sub(/^(lock|bnd)/, "", opcode)
				if (opcode ~ /^(j[a-z]+|loop[a-z]*|call|ret)/) {
					clear_provenance("")
					continue
				}
				if (opcode ~ /^mov[a-z]*\$0x[0-9a-f]+,%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					source = opcode
					sub(/^mov[a-z]*\$0x/, "", source)
					sub(/,.*/, "", source)
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					destination_width = width(destination)
					destination = canonical(destination)
					if (destination != "") {
						if (destination_width >= 32)
							value[destination] = source
						else
							value[destination] = "dynamic"
					}
				} else if (opcode ~ /^mov[a-z]*%[a-z0-9]+,%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					source = opcode
					sub(/^mov[a-z]*/, "", source)
					sub(/,.*/, "", source)
					source_width = width(source)
					source = canonical(source)
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					destination_width = width(destination)
					destination = canonical(destination)
					if (destination != "") {
						if (destination_width >= 32 && source_width >= 32 &&
						    source in value)
							value[destination] = value[source]
						else
							value[destination] = "dynamic"
					}
				} else if (opcode ~ /^(xchg|xadd)/) {
					source = opcode
					sub(/^[^%]*/, "%", source)
					sub(/,.*/, "", source)
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(source))
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
				} else if (opcode ~ /^mulx[a-z]*%[a-z0-9]+,%[a-z0-9]+,%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^[^,]*,/, "", destination)
					sub(/,.*/, "", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
				} else if (opcode ~ /^(rdfsbase|rdgsbase|rdpid|rdsspq)[a-z]*%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^[^%]*/, "%", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
				} else if (opcode ~ /^(pop|inc|dec|neg|not|bswap|set[a-z]+|rol|ror|rcl|rcr|sal|sar|shl|shr|rdrand|rdseed)[a-z]*%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^[^%]*/, "%", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
				} else if (opcode ~ /,%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					destination = canonical(destination)
					clear_provenance(destination)
				}
				clear_implicit_destinations(opcode)
				if (opcode !~ /^mov/ && opcode !~ /^(cpuid|getsec|rdtscp)/ &&
				     opcode ~ /%((r|e)?cx|c[lh])/)
					clear_provenance("rcx")
				if (opcode ~ /wrmsr/) {
					if (!("rcx" in value))
						print "unknown"
					else
						print value["rcx"]
					clear_provenance("")
				}
			}
		} /home/sean/tmp.kKLmThfAK0/disassembly-21

		function canonical(register_name, name) {
			name = register_name
			gsub(/^%/, "", name)
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(ax|al|ah)$/) return "rax"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(bx|bl|bh)$/) return "rbx"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(cx|cl|ch)$/) return "rcx"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(dx|dl|dh)$/) return "rdx"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(si|sil)$/) return "rsi"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(di|dil)$/) return "rdi"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(bp|bpl)$/) return "rbp"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(sp|spl)$/) return "rsp"
			if (name ~ /^r(8|9|1[0-5])(d|w|b)?$/) {
				sub(/[dwb]$/, "", name)
				return name
			}
			return ""
		}
		function width(register_name, name) {
			name = register_name
			gsub(/^%/, "", name)
			if (name ~ /^r(ax|bx|cx|dx|si|di|bp|sp)$/ ||
			    name ~ /^r(8|9|1[0-5])$/)
				return 64
			if (name ~ /^e(ax|bx|cx|dx|si|di|bp|sp)$/ ||
			    name ~ /^r(8|9|1[0-5])d$/)
				return 32
			if (name ~ /^(ax|bx|cx|dx|si|di|bp|sp)$/ ||
			    name ~ /^r(8|9|1[0-5])w$/)
				return 16
			return 8
		}
		function clear_provenance(register_name) {
			if (register_name == "") {
				for (name in value)
					delete value[name]
			} else {
				delete value[register_name]
			}
		}
		function is_rdpru(instruction) {
			while (instruction ~ /^(addr16|addr32|cs|ds|es|fs|gs|ss)/ ||
			       instruction ~ /^rex(\.[WRXB]+)?/) {
				sub(/^(addr16|addr32|cs|ds|es|fs|gs|ss)/, "", instruction)
				sub(/^rex(\.[WRXB]+)?/, "", instruction)
			}
			return instruction == "rdpru"
		}

		function canonical(register_name, name) {
			name = register_name
			gsub(/^%/, "", name)
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(ax|al|ah)$/) return "rax"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(bx|bl|bh)$/) return "rbx"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(cx|cl|ch)$/) return "rcx"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(dx|dl|dh)$/) return "rdx"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(si|sil)$/) return "rsi"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(di|dil)$/) return "rdi"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(bp|bpl)$/) return "rbp"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(sp|spl)$/) return "rsp"
			if (name ~ /^r(8|9|1[0-5])(d|w|b)?$/) {
				sub(/[dwb]$/, "", name)
				return name
			}
			return ""
		}
		function width(register_name, name) {
			name = register_name
			gsub(/^%/, "", name)
			if (name ~ /^r(ax|bx|cx|dx|si|di|bp|sp)$/ ||
			    name ~ /^r(8|9|1[0-5])$/)
				return 64
			if (name ~ /^e(ax|bx|cx|dx|si|di|bp|sp)$/ ||
			    name ~ /^r(8|9|1[0-5])d$/)
				return 32
			if (name ~ /^(ax|bx|cx|dx|si|di|bp|sp)$/ ||
			    name ~ /^r(8|9|1[0-5])w$/)
				return 16
			return 8
		}
		function clear_provenance(register_name) {
			if (register_name == "") {
				for (name in value)
					delete value[name]
			} else {
				delete value[register_name]
			}
		}
		function is_rdpru(instruction) {
			while (instruction ~ /^(addr16|addr32|cs|ds|es|fs|gs|ss)/ ||
			       instruction ~ /^rex(\.[WRXB]+)?/) {
				sub(/^(addr16|addr32|cs|ds|es|fs|gs|ss)/, "", instruction)
				sub(/^rex(\.[WRXB]+)?/, "", instruction)
			}
			return instruction == "rdpru"
		}
		function clear_implicit_destinations(instruction, operands) {
			operands = instruction
			gsub(/^[a-z0-9]+/, "", operands)
			if (instruction ~ /^(mul|div|idiv)[a-z]*%/ ||
			    (instruction ~ /^imul[a-z]*%/ && operands !~ /,/)) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(cbw|cwde|cdqe)$/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(cwd|cdq|cqo)$/) {
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(cpuid|getsec)$/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rbx")
				clear_provenance("rcx")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(rdmsr|rdtsc|xgetbv)$/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(rdpmc|rdpkru)$/ || is_rdpru(instruction)) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^rdtscp$/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rcx")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^cmpxchg(8b|16b)/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^cmpxchg/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(lahf|xlat)/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(lods)/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rsi")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(movs|cmps)/) {
				clear_provenance("rsi")
				clear_provenance("rdi")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(stos|scas|ins)/) {
				clear_provenance("rdi")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^outs/) {
				clear_provenance("rsi")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(enter|leave|push|pop)/) {
				clear_provenance("rsp")
				if (instruction ~ /^(enter|leave)/)
					clear_provenance("rbp")
			}
			if (instruction ~ /^(loop|loope|loopne)/)
				clear_provenance("rcx")
			if (instruction ~ /^(rsm|syscall|sysret|rep)/)
				clear_provenance("")
		}
		function normalize(address) {
			sub(/^0x/, "", address)
			sub(/^0+/, "", address)
			return address == "" ? "0" : address
		}
		{
			line[NR] = $0
			if ($0 ~ /\t((lock|bnd)[[:space:]]+)?(j[a-z]+|loop[a-z]*)[[:space:]]+(0x)?[0-9a-f]+/) {
				destination = $0
				sub(/^.*\t((lock|bnd)[[:space:]]+)?(j[a-z]+|loop[a-z]*)[[:space:]]+/, "", destination)
				sub(/[[:space:]].*$/, "", destination)
				branch_target[normalize(destination)] = 1
			}
		}
		END {
			for (number = 1; number <= NR; number++) {
				text = line[number]
				if (text ~ /^Disassembly of section /) {
					clear_provenance("")
					continue
				}
				if (text ~ /^[0-9a-f]+ <[^>]+>:/) {
					clear_provenance("")
					continue
				}
				if (text !~ /^[[:space:]]*[0-9a-f]+:/)
					continue
				address = text
				sub(/^[[:space:]]*/, "", address)
				sub(/:.*/, "", address)
				address = normalize(address)
				if (address in branch_target)
					clear_provenance("")
				instruction = text
				sub(/^.*\t/, "", instruction)
				gsub(/[[:space:]]+/, "", instruction)
				opcode = instruction
				sub(/^(lock|bnd)/, "", opcode)
				if (opcode ~ /^(j[a-z]+|loop[a-z]*|call|ret)/) {
					clear_provenance("")
					continue
				}
				if (opcode ~ /^mov[a-z]*\$0x[0-9a-f]+,%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					source = opcode
					sub(/^mov[a-z]*\$0x/, "", source)
					sub(/,.*/, "", source)
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					destination_width = width(destination)
					destination = canonical(destination)
					if (destination != "") {
						if (destination_width >= 32)
							value[destination] = source
						else
							value[destination] = "dynamic"
					}
				} else if (opcode ~ /^mov[a-z]*%[a-z0-9]+,%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					source = opcode
					sub(/^mov[a-z]*/, "", source)
					sub(/,.*/, "", source)
					source_width = width(source)
					source = canonical(source)
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					destination_width = width(destination)
					destination = canonical(destination)
					if (destination != "") {
						if (destination_width >= 32 && source_width >= 32 &&
						    source in value)
							value[destination] = value[source]
						else
							value[destination] = "dynamic"
					}
				} else if (opcode ~ /^(xchg|xadd)/) {
					source = opcode
					sub(/^[^%]*/, "%", source)
					sub(/,.*/, "", source)
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(source))
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
				} else if (opcode ~ /^mulx[a-z]*%[a-z0-9]+,%[a-z0-9]+,%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^[^,]*,/, "", destination)
					sub(/,.*/, "", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
				} else if (opcode ~ /^(rdfsbase|rdgsbase|rdpid|rdsspq)[a-z]*%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^[^%]*/, "%", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
				} else if (opcode ~ /^(pop|inc|dec|neg|not|bswap|set[a-z]+|rol|ror|rcl|rcr|sal|sar|shl|shr|rdrand|rdseed)[a-z]*%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^[^%]*/, "%", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
				} else if (opcode ~ /,%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					destination = canonical(destination)
					clear_provenance(destination)
				}
				clear_implicit_destinations(opcode)
				if (opcode !~ /^mov/ && opcode !~ /^(cpuid|getsec|rdtscp)/ &&
				     opcode ~ /%((r|e)?cx|c[lh])/)
					clear_provenance("rcx")
				if (opcode ~ /wrmsr/) {
					if (!("rcx" in value))
						print "unknown"
					else
						print value["rcx"]
					clear_provenance("")
				}
			}
		} /home/sean/tmp.kKLmThfAK0/disassembly-23

		function canonical(register_name, name) {
			name = register_name
			gsub(/^%/, "", name)
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(ax|al|ah)$/) return "rax"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(bx|bl|bh)$/) return "rbx"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(cx|cl|ch)$/) return "rcx"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(dx|dl|dh)$/) return "rdx"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(si|sil)$/) return "rsi"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(di|dil)$/) return "rdi"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(bp|bpl)$/) return "rbp"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(sp|spl)$/) return "rsp"
			if (name ~ /^r(8|9|1[0-5])(d|w|b)?$/) {
				sub(/[dwb]$/, "", name)
				return name
			}
			return ""
		}
		function width(register_name, name) {
			name = register_name
			gsub(/^%/, "", name)
			if (name ~ /^r(ax|bx|cx|dx|si|di|bp|sp)$/ ||
			    name ~ /^r(8|9|1[0-5])$/)
				return 64
			if (name ~ /^e(ax|bx|cx|dx|si|di|bp|sp)$/ ||
			    name ~ /^r(8|9|1[0-5])d$/)
				return 32
			if (name ~ /^(ax|bx|cx|dx|si|di|bp|sp)$/ ||
			    name ~ /^r(8|9|1[0-5])w$/)
				return 16
			return 8
		}
		function clear_provenance(register_name) {
			if (register_name == "") {
				for (name in value)
					delete value[name]
			} else {
				delete value[register_name]
			}
		}
		function is_rdpru(instruction) {
			while (instruction ~ /^(addr16|addr32|cs|ds|es|fs|gs|ss)/ ||
			       instruction ~ /^rex(\.[WRXB]+)?/) {
				sub(/^(addr16|addr32|cs|ds|es|fs|gs|ss)/, "", instruction)
				sub(/^rex(\.[WRXB]+)?/, "", instruction)
			}
			return instruction == "rdpru"
		}
		function clear_implicit_destinations(instruction, operands) {
			operands = instruction
			gsub(/^[a-z0-9]+/, "", operands)
			if (instruction ~ /^(mul|div|idiv)[a-z]*%/ ||
			    (instruction ~ /^imul[a-z]*%/ && operands !~ /,/)) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(cbw|cwde|cdqe)$/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(cwd|cdq|cqo)$/) {
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(cpuid|getsec)$/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rbx")
				clear_provenance("rcx")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(rdmsr|rdtsc|xgetbv)$/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(rdpmc|rdpkru)$/ || is_rdpru(instruction)) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^rdtscp$/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rcx")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^cmpxchg(8b|16b)/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^cmpxchg/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(lahf|xlat)/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(lods)/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rsi")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(movs|cmps)/) {
				clear_provenance("rsi")
				clear_provenance("rdi")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(stos|scas|ins)/) {
				clear_provenance("rdi")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^outs/) {
				clear_provenance("rsi")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(enter|leave|push|pop)/) {
				clear_provenance("rsp")
				if (instruction ~ /^(enter|leave)/)
					clear_provenance("rbp")
			}
			if (instruction ~ /^(loop|loope|loopne)/)
				clear_provenance("rcx")
			if (instruction ~ /^(rsm|syscall|sysret|rep)/)
				clear_provenance("")
		}
		function normalize(address) {
			sub(/^0x/, "", address)
			sub(/^0+/, "", address)
			return address == "" ? "0" : address
		}
		{
			line[NR] = $0
			if ($0 ~ /\t((lock|bnd)[[:space:]]+)?(j[a-z]+|loop[a-z]*)[[:space:]]+(0x)?[0-9a-f]+/) {
				destination = $0
				sub(/^.*\t((lock|bnd)[[:space:]]+)?(j[a-z]+|loop[a-z]*)[[:space:]]+/, "", destination)
				sub(/[[:space:]].*$/, "", destination)
				branch_target[normalize(destination)] = 1
			}
		}
		END {
			for (number = 1; number <= NR; number++) {
				text = line[number]
				if (text ~ /^Disassembly of section /) {
					clear_provenance("")
					continue
				}
				if (text ~ /^[0-9a-f]+ <[^>]+>:/) {
					clear_provenance("")
					continue
				}
				if (text !~ /^[[:space:]]*[0-9a-f]+:/)
					continue
				address = text
				sub(/^[[:space:]]*/, "", address)
				sub(/:.*/, "", address)
				address = normalize(address)
				if (address in branch_target)
					clear_provenance("")
				instruction = text
				sub(/^.*\t/, "", instruction)
				gsub(/[[:space:]]+/, "", instruction)
				opcode = instruction
				sub(/^(lock|bnd)/, "", opcode)
				if (opcode ~ /^(j[a-z]+|loop[a-z]*|call|ret)/) {
					clear_provenance("")
					continue
				}
				if (opcode ~ /^mov[a-z]*\$0x[0-9a-f]+,%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					source = opcode
					sub(/^mov[a-z]*\$0x/, "", source)
					sub(/,.*/, "", source)
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					destination_width = width(destination)
					destination = canonical(destination)
					if (destination != "") {
						if (destination_width >= 32)
							value[destination] = source
						else
							value[destination] = "dynamic"
					}
				} else if (opcode ~ /^mov[a-z]*%[a-z0-9]+,%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					source = opcode
					sub(/^mov[a-z]*/, "", source)
					sub(/,.*/, "", source)
					source_width = width(source)
					source = canonical(source)
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					destination_width = width(destination)
					destination = canonical(destination)
					if (destination != "") {
						if (destination_width >= 32 && source_width >= 32 &&
						    source in value)
							value[destination] = value[source]
						else
							value[destination] = "dynamic"
					}
				} else if (opcode ~ /^(xchg|xadd)/) {
					source = opcode
					sub(/^[^%]*/, "%", source)
					sub(/,.*/, "", source)
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(source))
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
				} else if (opcode ~ /^mulx[a-z]*%[a-z0-9]+,%[a-z0-9]+,%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^[^,]*,/, "", destination)
					sub(/,.*/, "", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
				} else if (opcode ~ /^(rdfsbase|rdgsbase|rdpid|rdsspq)[a-z]*%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^[^%]*/, "%", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
				} else if (opcode ~ /^(pop|inc|dec|neg|not|bswap|set[a-z]+|rol|ror|rcl|rcr|sal|sar|shl|shr|rdrand|rdseed)[a-z]*%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^[^%]*/, "%", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
				} else if (opcode ~ /,%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					destination = canonical(destination)
					clear_provenance(destination)
				}
				clear_implicit_destinations(opcode)
				if (opcode !~ /^mov/ && opcode !~ /^(cpuid|getsec|rdtscp)/ &&
				     opcode ~ /%((r|e)?cx|c[lh])/)
					clear_provenance("rcx")
				if (opcode ~ /wrmsr/) {
					if (!("rcx" in value))
						print "unknown"
					else
						print value["rcx"]
					clear_provenance("")
				}
			}
		} /home/sean/tmp.kKLmThfAK0/disassembly-25

					clear_provenance("")

		} /home/sean/tmp.kKLmThfAK0/disassembly-26

+ awk 
		function canonical(register_name, name) {
			name = register_name
			gsub(/^%/, "", name)
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(ax|al|ah)$/) return "rax"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(bx|bl|bh)$/) return "rbx"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(cx|cl|ch)$/) return "rcx"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(dx|dl|dh)$/) return "rdx"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(si|sil)$/) return "rsi"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(di|dil)$/) return "rdi"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(bp|bpl)$/) return "rbp"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(sp|spl)$/) return "rsp"
			if (name ~ /^r(8|9|1[0-5])(d|w|b)?$/) {
				sub(/[dwb]$/, "", name)
				return name
			}
			return ""
		}
		function width(register_name, name) {
			name = register_name
			gsub(/^%/, "", name)
			if (name ~ /^r(ax|bx|cx|dx|si|di|bp|sp)$/ ||
			    name ~ /^r(8|9|1[0-5])$/)
				return 64
			if (name ~ /^e(ax|bx|cx|dx|si|di|bp|sp)$/ ||
			    name ~ /^r(8|9|1[0-5])d$/)
				return 32
			if (name ~ /^(ax|bx|cx|dx|si|di|bp|sp)$/ ||
			    name ~ /^r(8|9|1[0-5])w$/)
				return 16
			return 8
		}
		function clear_provenance(register_name) {
			if (register_name == "") {
				for (name in value)
					delete value[name]
			} else {
				delete value[register_name]
			}
		}
		function is_rdpru(instruction) {
			while (instruction ~ /^(addr16|addr32|cs|ds|es|fs|gs|ss)/ ||
			       instruction ~ /^rex(\.[WRXB]+)?/) {
				sub(/^(addr16|addr32|cs|ds|es|fs|gs|ss)/, "", instruction)
				sub(/^rex(\.[WRXB]+)?/, "", instruction)
			}
			return instruction == "rdpru"
		}
		function clear_implicit_destinations(instruction, operands) {
			operands = instruction
			gsub(/^[a-z0-9]+/, "", operands)
			if (instruction ~ /^(mul|div|idiv)[a-z]*%/ ||
			    (instruction ~ /^imul[a-z]*%/ && operands !~ /,/)) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(cbw|cwde|cdqe)$/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(cwd|cdq|cqo)$/) {
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(cpuid|getsec)$/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rbx")
				clear_provenance("rcx")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(rdmsr|rdtsc|xgetbv)$/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(rdpmc|rdpkru)$/ || is_rdpru(instruction)) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^rdtscp$/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rcx")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^cmpxchg(8b|16b)/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^cmpxchg/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(lahf|xlat)/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(lods)/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rsi")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(movs|cmps)/) {
				clear_provenance("rsi")
				clear_provenance("rdi")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(stos|scas|ins)/) {
				clear_provenance("rdi")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^outs/) {
				clear_provenance("rsi")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(enter|leave|push|pop)/) {
				clear_provenance("rsp")
				if (instruction ~ /^(enter|leave)/)
					clear_provenance("rbp")
			}
			if (instruction ~ /^(loop|loope|loopne)/)
				clear_provenance("rcx")
			if (instruction ~ /^(rsm|syscall|sysret|rep)/)
				clear_provenance("")
		}
		function normalize(address) {
			sub(/^0x/, "", address)
			sub(/^0+/, "", address)
			return address == "" ? "0" : address
		}
		{
			line[NR] = $0
			if ($0 ~ /\t((lock|bnd)[[:space:]]+)?(j[a-z]+|loop[a-z]*)[[:space:]]+(0x)?[0-9a-f]+/) {
				destination = $0
				sub(/^.*\t((lock|bnd)[[:space:]]+)?(j[a-z]+|loop[a-z]*)[[:space:]]+/, "", destination)
				sub(/[[:space:]].*$/, "", destination)
				branch_target[normalize(destination)] = 1
			}
		}
		END {
			for (number = 1; number <= NR; number++) {
				text = line[number]
				if (text ~ /^Disassembly of section /) {
					clear_provenance("")
					continue
				}
				if (text ~ /^[0-9a-f]+ <[^>]+>:/) {
					clear_provenance("")
					continue
				}
				if (text !~ /^[[:space:]]*[0-9a-f]+:/)
					continue
				address = text
				sub(/^[[:space:]]*/, "", address)
				sub(/:.*/, "", address)
				address = normalize(address)
				if (address in branch_target)
					clear_provenance("")
				instruction = text
				sub(/^.*\t/, "", instruction)
				gsub(/[[:space:]]+/, "", instruction)
				opcode = instruction
				sub(/^(lock|bnd)/, "", opcode)
				if (opcode ~ /^(j[a-z]+|loop[a-z]*|call|ret)/) {
					clear_provenance("")
					continue
				}
				if (opcode ~ /^mov[a-z]*\$0x[0-9a-f]+,%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					source = opcode
					sub(/^mov[a-z]*\$0x/, "", source)
					sub(/,.*/, "", source)
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					destination_width = width(destination)
					destination = canonical(destination)
					if (destination != "") {
						if (destination_width >= 32)
							value[destination] = source
						else
							value[destination] = "dynamic"
					}
				} else if (opcode ~ /^mov[a-z]*%[a-z0-9]+,%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					source = opcode
					sub(/^mov[a-z]*/, "", source)
					sub(/,.*/, "", source)
					source_width = width(source)
					source = canonical(source)
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					destination_width = width(destination)
					destination = canonical(destination)
					if (destination != "") {
						if (destination_width >= 32 && source_width >= 32 &&
						    source in value)
							value[destination] = value[source]
						else
							value[destination] = "dynamic"
					}
				} else if (opcode ~ /^(xchg|xadd)/) {
					source = opcode
					sub(/^[^%]*/, "%", source)
					sub(/,.*/, "", source)
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(source))
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
				} else if (opcode ~ /^mulx[a-z]*%[a-z0-9]+,%[a-z0-9]+,%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^[^,]*,/, "", destination)
					sub(/,.*/, "", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
				} else if (opcode ~ /^(rdfsbase|rdgsbase|rdpid|rdsspq)[a-z]*%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^[^%]*/, "%", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
				} else if (opcode ~ /^(pop|inc|dec|neg|not|bswap|set[a-z]+|rol|ror|rcl|rcr|sal|sar|shl|shr|rdrand|rdseed)[a-z]*%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^[^%]*/, "%", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
				} else if (opcode ~ /,%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					destination = canonical(destination)
					clear_provenance(destination)
				}
				clear_implicit_destinations(opcode)
				if (opcode !~ /^mov/ && opcode !~ /^(cpuid|getsec|rdtscp)/ &&
				     opcode ~ /%((r|e)?cx|c[lh])/)
					clear_provenance("rcx")
				if (opcode ~ /wrmsr/) {
					if (!("rcx" in value))
						print "unknown"
					else
						print value["rcx"]
					clear_provenance("")
				}
			}
		} /home/sean/tmp.kKLmThfAK0/disassembly-27

					sub(/^[^%]*/, "%", source)

					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(source))
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
				} else if (opcode ~ /^mulx[a-z]*%[a-z0-9]+,%[a-z0-9]+,%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^[^,]*,/, "", destination)
					sub(/,.*/, "", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
				} else if (opcode ~ /^(rdfsbase|rdgsbase|rdpid|rdsspq)[a-z]*%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^[^%]*/, "%", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))

					destination = opcode
					sub(/^[^%]*/, "%", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
				} else if (opcode ~ /,%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					destination = canonical(destination)
					clear_provenance(destination)
				}
				clear_implicit_destinations(opcode)
				if (opcode !~ /^mov/ && opcode !~ /^(cpuid|getsec|rdtscp)/ &&
				     opcode ~ /%((r|e)?cx|c[lh])/)
					clear_provenance("rcx")

					if (!("rcx" in value))
						print "unknown"
					else
						print value["rcx"]
					clear_provenance("")
				}
			}

+ awk 
		function canonical(register_name, name) {
			name = register_name
			gsub(/^%/, "", name)
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(ax|al|ah)$/) return "rax"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(bx|bl|bh)$/) return "rbx"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(cx|cl|ch)$/) return "rcx"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(dx|dl|dh)$/) return "rdx"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(si|sil)$/) return "rsi"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(di|dil)$/) return "rdi"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(bp|bpl)$/) return "rbp"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(sp|spl)$/) return "rsp"
			if (name ~ /^r(8|9|1[0-5])(d|w|b)?$/) {
				sub(/[dwb]$/, "", name)
				return name
			}
			return ""
		}
		function width(register_name, name) {
			name = register_name
			gsub(/^%/, "", name)
			if (name ~ /^r(ax|bx|cx|dx|si|di|bp|sp)$/ ||
			    name ~ /^r(8|9|1[0-5])$/)
				return 64
			if (name ~ /^e(ax|bx|cx|dx|si|di|bp|sp)$/ ||
			    name ~ /^r(8|9|1[0-5])d$/)
				return 32
			if (name ~ /^(ax|bx|cx|dx|si|di|bp|sp)$/ ||
			    name ~ /^r(8|9|1[0-5])w$/)
				return 16
			return 8
		}
		function clear_provenance(register_name) {
			if (register_name == "") {
				for (name in value)
					delete value[name]
			} else {
				delete value[register_name]
			}
		}
		function is_rdpru(instruction) {
			while (instruction ~ /^(addr16|addr32|cs|ds|es|fs|gs|ss)/ ||
			       instruction ~ /^rex(\.[WRXB]+)?/) {
				sub(/^(addr16|addr32|cs|ds|es|fs|gs|ss)/, "", instruction)
				sub(/^rex(\.[WRXB]+)?/, "", instruction)
			}
			return instruction == "rdpru"
		}
		function clear_implicit_destinations(instruction, operands) {
			operands = instruction
			gsub(/^[a-z0-9]+/, "", operands)
			if (instruction ~ /^(mul|div|idiv)[a-z]*%/ ||
			    (instruction ~ /^imul[a-z]*%/ && operands !~ /,/)) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(cbw|cwde|cdqe)$/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(cwd|cdq|cqo)$/) {
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(cpuid|getsec)$/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rbx")
				clear_provenance("rcx")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(rdmsr|rdtsc|xgetbv)$/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(rdpmc|rdpkru)$/ || is_rdpru(instruction)) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^rdtscp$/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rcx")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^cmpxchg(8b|16b)/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^cmpxchg/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(lahf|xlat)/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(lods)/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rsi")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(movs|cmps)/) {
				clear_provenance("rsi")
				clear_provenance("rdi")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(stos|scas|ins)/) {
				clear_provenance("rdi")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^outs/) {
				clear_provenance("rsi")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(enter|leave|push|pop)/) {
				clear_provenance("rsp")
				if (instruction ~ /^(enter|leave)/)
					clear_provenance("rbp")
			}
			if (instruction ~ /^(loop|loope|loopne)/)
				clear_provenance("rcx")
			if (instruction ~ /^(rsm|syscall|sysret|rep)/)
				clear_provenance("")
		}
		function normalize(address) {
			sub(/^0x/, "", address)
			sub(/^0+/, "", address)
			return address == "" ? "0" : address
		}
		{
			line[NR] = $0
			if ($0 ~ /\t((lock|bnd)[[:space:]]+)?(j[a-z]+|loop[a-z]*)[[:space:]]+(0x)?[0-9a-f]+/) {
				destination = $0
				sub(/^.*\t((lock|bnd)[[:space:]]+)?(j[a-z]+|loop[a-z]*)[[:space:]]+/, "", destination)
				sub(/[[:space:]].*$/, "", destination)
				branch_target[normalize(destination)] = 1
			}
		}
		END {
			for (number = 1; number <= NR; number++) {
				text = line[number]
				if (text ~ /^Disassembly of section /) {
					clear_provenance("")
					continue
				}
				if (text ~ /^[0-9a-f]+ <[^>]+>:/) {
					clear_provenance("")
					continue
				}
				if (text !~ /^[[:space:]]*[0-9a-f]+:/)
					continue
				address = text
				sub(/^[[:space:]]*/, "", address)
				sub(/:.*/, "", address)
				address = normalize(address)
				if (address in branch_target)
					clear_provenance("")
				instruction = text
				sub(/^.*\t/, "", instruction)
				gsub(/[[:space:]]+/, "", instruction)
				opcode = instruction
				sub(/^(lock|bnd)/, "", opcode)
				if (opcode ~ /^(j[a-z]+|loop[a-z]*|call|ret)/) {
					clear_provenance("")
					continue
				}
				if (opcode ~ /^mov[a-z]*\$0x[0-9a-f]+,%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					source = opcode
					sub(/^mov[a-z]*\$0x/, "", source)
					sub(/,.*/, "", source)
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					destination_width = width(destination)
					destination = canonical(destination)
					if (destination != "") {
						if (destination_width >= 32)
							value[destination] = source
						else
							value[destination] = "dynamic"
					}
				} else if (opcode ~ /^mov[a-z]*%[a-z0-9]+,%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					source = opcode
					sub(/^mov[a-z]*/, "", source)
					sub(/,.*/, "", source)
					source_width = width(source)
					source = canonical(source)
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					destination_width = width(destination)
					destination = canonical(destination)
					if (destination != "") {
						if (destination_width >= 32 && source_width >= 32 &&
						    source in value)
							value[destination] = value[source]
						else
							value[destination] = "dynamic"
					}
				} else if (opcode ~ /^(xchg|xadd)/) {
					source = opcode
					sub(/^[^%]*/, "%", source)
					sub(/,.*/, "", source)
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(source))
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
				} else if (opcode ~ /^mulx[a-z]*%[a-z0-9]+,%[a-z0-9]+,%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^[^,]*,/, "", destination)
					sub(/,.*/, "", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
				} else if (opcode ~ /^(rdfsbase|rdgsbase|rdpid|rdsspq)[a-z]*%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^[^%]*/, "%", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
				} else if (opcode ~ /^(pop|inc|dec|neg|not|bswap|set[a-z]+|rol|ror|rcl|rcr|sal|sar|shl|shr|rdrand|rdseed)[a-z]*%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^[^%]*/, "%", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
				} else if (opcode ~ /,%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					destination = canonical(destination)
					clear_provenance(destination)
				}
				clear_implicit_destinations(opcode)
				if (opcode !~ /^mov/ && opcode !~ /^(cpuid|getsec|rdtscp)/ &&
				     opcode ~ /%((r|e)?cx|c[lh])/)
					clear_provenance("rcx")
				if (opcode ~ /wrmsr/) {
					if (!("rcx" in value))
						print "unknown"
					else
						print value["rcx"]
					clear_provenance("")
				}
			}
		} /home/sean/tmp.kKLmThfAK0/disassembly-29

					continue
				address = text
				sub(/^[[:space:]]*/, "", address)
				sub(/:.*/, "", address)
				address = normalize(address)
				if (address in branch_target)
					clear_provenance("")
				instruction = text
				sub(/^.*\t/, "", instruction)
				gsub(/[[:space:]]+/, "", instruction)
				opcode = instruction
				sub(/^(lock|bnd)/, "", opcode)
				if (opcode ~ /^(j[a-z]+|loop[a-z]*|call|ret)/) {
					clear_provenance("")
					continue
				}
				if (opcode ~ /^mov[a-z]*\$0x[0-9a-f]+,%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					source = opcode
					sub(/^mov[a-z]*\$0x/, "", source)
					sub(/,.*/, "", source)
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					destination_width = width(destination)
					destination = canonical(destination)
					if (destination != "") {
						if (destination_width >= 32)
							value[destination] = source
						else
							value[destination] = "dynamic"
					}
				} else if (opcode ~ /^mov[a-z]*%[a-z0-9]+,%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					source = opcode
					sub(/^mov[a-z]*/, "", source)
					sub(/,.*/, "", source)
					source_width = width(source)
					source = canonical(source)
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					destination_width = width(destination)
					destination = canonical(destination)
					if (destination != "") {
						if (destination_width >= 32 && source_width >= 32 &&
						    source in value)
							value[destination] = value[source]
						else
							value[destination] = "dynamic"
					}
				} else if (opcode ~ /^(xchg|xadd)/) {
					source = opcode
					sub(/^[^%]*/, "%", source)
					sub(/,.*/, "", source)
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(source))
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
				} else if (opcode ~ /^mulx[a-z]*%[a-z0-9]+,%[a-z0-9]+,%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^[^,]*,/, "", destination)
					sub(/,.*/, "", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
				} else if (opcode ~ /^(rdfsbase|rdgsbase|rdpid|rdsspq)[a-z]*%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^[^%]*/, "%", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
				} else if (opcode ~ /^(pop|inc|dec|neg|not|bswap|set[a-z]+|rol|ror|rcl|rcr|sal|sar|shl|shr|rdrand|rdseed)[a-z]*%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^[^%]*/, "%", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))

					destination = opcode

					destination = canonical(destination)
					clear_provenance(destination)
				}

				if (opcode !~ /^mov/ && opcode !~ /^(cpuid|getsec|rdtscp)/ &&
				     opcode ~ /%((r|e)?cx|c[lh])/)
					clear_provenance("rcx")
				if (opcode ~ /wrmsr/) {
					if (!("rcx" in value))
						print "unknown"
					else
						print value["rcx"]
					clear_provenance("")
				}
			}

		function canonical(register_name, name) {
			name = register_name
			gsub(/^%/, "", name)
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(ax|al|ah)$/) return "rax"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(bx|bl|bh)$/) return "rbx"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(cx|cl|ch)$/) return "rcx"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(dx|dl|dh)$/) return "rdx"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(si|sil)$/) return "rsi"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(di|dil)$/) return "rdi"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(bp|bpl)$/) return "rbp"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(sp|spl)$/) return "rsp"
			if (name ~ /^r(8|9|1[0-5])(d|w|b)?$/) {
				sub(/[dwb]$/, "", name)
				return name
			}
			return ""
		}
		function width(register_name, name) {
			name = register_name
			gsub(/^%/, "", name)
			if (name ~ /^r(ax|bx|cx|dx|si|di|bp|sp)$/ ||
			    name ~ /^r(8|9|1[0-5])$/)
				return 64
			if (name ~ /^e(ax|bx|cx|dx|si|di|bp|sp)$/ ||
			    name ~ /^r(8|9|1[0-5])d$/)
				return 32
			if (name ~ /^(ax|bx|cx|dx|si|di|bp|sp)$/ ||
			    name ~ /^r(8|9|1[0-5])w$/)
				return 16
			return 8
		}
		function clear_provenance(register_name) {
			if (register_name == "") {
				for (name in value)
					delete value[name]
			} else {
				delete value[register_name]
			}
		}
		function is_rdpru(instruction) {
			while (instruction ~ /^(addr16|addr32|cs|ds|es|fs|gs|ss)/ ||
			       instruction ~ /^rex(\.[WRXB]+)?/) {
				sub(/^(addr16|addr32|cs|ds|es|fs|gs|ss)/, "", instruction)
				sub(/^rex(\.[WRXB]+)?/, "", instruction)
			}
			return instruction == "rdpru"
		}
		function clear_implicit_destinations(instruction, operands) {
			operands = instruction
			gsub(/^[a-z0-9]+/, "", operands)
			if (instruction ~ /^(mul|div|idiv)[a-z]*%/ ||
			    (instruction ~ /^imul[a-z]*%/ && operands !~ /,/)) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(cbw|cwde|cdqe)$/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(cwd|cdq|cqo)$/) {
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(cpuid|getsec)$/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rbx")
				clear_provenance("rcx")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(rdmsr|rdtsc|xgetbv)$/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(rdpmc|rdpkru)$/ || is_rdpru(instruction)) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^rdtscp$/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rcx")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^cmpxchg(8b|16b)/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^cmpxchg/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(lahf|xlat)/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(lods)/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rsi")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(movs|cmps)/) {
				clear_provenance("rsi")
				clear_provenance("rdi")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(stos|scas|ins)/) {
				clear_provenance("rdi")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^outs/) {
				clear_provenance("rsi")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(enter|leave|push|pop)/) {
				clear_provenance("rsp")
				if (instruction ~ /^(enter|leave)/)
					clear_provenance("rbp")
			}
			if (instruction ~ /^(loop|loope|loopne)/)
				clear_provenance("rcx")
			if (instruction ~ /^(rsm|syscall|sysret|rep)/)
				clear_provenance("")
		}
		function normalize(address) {
			sub(/^0x/, "", address)
			sub(/^0+/, "", address)
			return address == "" ? "0" : address
		}
		{
			line[NR] = $0
			if ($0 ~ /\t((lock|bnd)[[:space:]]+)?(j[a-z]+|loop[a-z]*)[[:space:]]+(0x)?[0-9a-f]+/) {
				destination = $0
				sub(/^.*\t((lock|bnd)[[:space:]]+)?(j[a-z]+|loop[a-z]*)[[:space:]]+/, "", destination)
				sub(/[[:space:]].*$/, "", destination)
				branch_target[normalize(destination)] = 1
			}
		}
		END {
			for (number = 1; number <= NR; number++) {
				text = line[number]
				if (text ~ /^Disassembly of section /) {
					clear_provenance("")
					continue
				}
				if (text ~ /^[0-9a-f]+ <[^>]+>:/) {
					clear_provenance("")
					continue
				}
				if (text !~ /^[[:space:]]*[0-9a-f]+:/)
					continue
				address = text
				sub(/^[[:space:]]*/, "", address)
				sub(/:.*/, "", address)
				address = normalize(address)
				if (address in branch_target)
					clear_provenance("")
				instruction = text
				sub(/^.*\t/, "", instruction)
				gsub(/[[:space:]]+/, "", instruction)
				opcode = instruction
				sub(/^(lock|bnd)/, "", opcode)
				if (opcode ~ /^(j[a-z]+|loop[a-z]*|call|ret)/) {
					clear_provenance("")
					continue
				}
				if (opcode ~ /^mov[a-z]*\$0x[0-9a-f]+,%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					source = opcode
					sub(/^mov[a-z]*\$0x/, "", source)
					sub(/,.*/, "", source)
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					destination_width = width(destination)
					destination = canonical(destination)
					if (destination != "") {
						if (destination_width >= 32)
							value[destination] = source
						else
							value[destination] = "dynamic"
					}
				} else if (opcode ~ /^mov[a-z]*%[a-z0-9]+,%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					source = opcode
					sub(/^mov[a-z]*/, "", source)
					sub(/,.*/, "", source)
					source_width = width(source)
					source = canonical(source)
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					destination_width = width(destination)
					destination = canonical(destination)
					if (destination != "") {
						if (destination_width >= 32 && source_width >= 32 &&
						    source in value)
							value[destination] = value[source]
						else
							value[destination] = "dynamic"
					}
				} else if (opcode ~ /^(xchg|xadd)/) {
					source = opcode
					sub(/^[^%]*/, "%", source)
					sub(/,.*/, "", source)
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(source))
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
				} else if (opcode ~ /^mulx[a-z]*%[a-z0-9]+,%[a-z0-9]+,%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^[^,]*,/, "", destination)
					sub(/,.*/, "", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
				} else if (opcode ~ /^(rdfsbase|rdgsbase|rdpid|rdsspq)[a-z]*%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^[^%]*/, "%", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
				} else if (opcode ~ /^(pop|inc|dec|neg|not|bswap|set[a-z]+|rol|ror|rcl|rcr|sal|sar|shl|shr|rdrand|rdseed)[a-z]*%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^[^%]*/, "%", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
				} else if (opcode ~ /,%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					destination = canonical(destination)
					clear_provenance(destination)
				}
				clear_implicit_destinations(opcode)

					clear_provenance("rcx")
				if (opcode ~ /wrmsr/) {
					if (!("rcx" in value))
						print "unknown"
					else
						print value["rcx"]
					clear_provenance("")
				}
			}

				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rsi")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(movs|cmps)/) {
				clear_provenance("rsi")
				clear_provenance("rdi")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(stos|scas|ins)/) {
				clear_provenance("rdi")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^outs/) {
				clear_provenance("rsi")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(enter|leave|push|pop)/) {
				clear_provenance("rsp")
				if (instruction ~ /^(enter|leave)/)
					clear_provenance("rbp")
			}
			if (instruction ~ /^(loop|loope|loopne)/)
				clear_provenance("rcx")
			if (instruction ~ /^(rsm|syscall|sysret|rep)/)
				clear_provenance("")
		}
		function normalize(address) {
			sub(/^0x/, "", address)
			sub(/^0+/, "", address)
			return address == "" ? "0" : address
		}
		{
			line[NR] = $0
			if ($0 ~ /\t((lock|bnd)[[:space:]]+)?(j[a-z]+|loop[a-z]*)[[:space:]]+(0x)?[0-9a-f]+/) {
				destination = $0
				sub(/^.*\t((lock|bnd)[[:space:]]+)?(j[a-z]+|loop[a-z]*)[[:space:]]+/, "", destination)
				sub(/[[:space:]].*$/, "", destination)
				branch_target[normalize(destination)] = 1
			}
		}
		END {
			for (number = 1; number <= NR; number++) {
				text = line[number]
				if (text ~ /^Disassembly of section /) {
					clear_provenance("")
					continue
				}
				if (text ~ /^[0-9a-f]+ <[^>]+>:/) {
					clear_provenance("")
					continue
				}
				if (text !~ /^[[:space:]]*[0-9a-f]+:/)
					continue
				address = text
				sub(/^[[:space:]]*/, "", address)
				sub(/:.*/, "", address)
				address = normalize(address)
				if (address in branch_target)
					clear_provenance("")
				instruction = text
				sub(/^.*\t/, "", instruction)
				gsub(/[[:space:]]+/, "", instruction)
				opcode = instruction
				sub(/^(lock|bnd)/, "", opcode)
				if (opcode ~ /^(j[a-z]+|loop[a-z]*|call|ret)/) {
					clear_provenance("")
					continue
				}
				if (opcode ~ /^mov[a-z]*\$0x[0-9a-f]+,%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					source = opcode
					sub(/^mov[a-z]*\$0x/, "", source)
					sub(/,.*/, "", source)
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					destination_width = width(destination)
					destination = canonical(destination)
					if (destination != "") {
						if (destination_width >= 32)
							value[destination] = source
						else
							value[destination] = "dynamic"
					}
				} else if (opcode ~ /^mov[a-z]*%[a-z0-9]+,%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					source = opcode
					sub(/^mov[a-z]*/, "", source)
					sub(/,.*/, "", source)
					source_width = width(source)
					source = canonical(source)
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					destination_width = width(destination)
					destination = canonical(destination)
					if (destination != "") {
						if (destination_width >= 32 && source_width >= 32 &&
						    source in value)
							value[destination] = value[source]
						else
							value[destination] = "dynamic"
					}
				} else if (opcode ~ /^(xchg|xadd)/) {
					source = opcode
					sub(/^[^%]*/, "%", source)
					sub(/,.*/, "", source)
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(source))
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
				} else if (opcode ~ /^mulx[a-z]*%[a-z0-9]+,%[a-z0-9]+,%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^[^,]*,/, "", destination)
					sub(/,.*/, "", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
				} else if (opcode ~ /^(rdfsbase|rdgsbase|rdpid|rdsspq)[a-z]*%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^[^%]*/, "%", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
				} else if (opcode ~ /^(pop|inc|dec|neg|not|bswap|set[a-z]+|rol|ror|rcl|rcr|sal|sar|shl|shr|rdrand|rdseed)[a-z]*%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^[^%]*/, "%", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
				} else if (opcode ~ /,%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					destination = canonical(destination)
					clear_provenance(destination)

				clear_implicit_destinations(opcode)
				if (opcode !~ /^mov/ && opcode !~ /^(cpuid|getsec|rdtscp)/ &&
				     opcode ~ /%((r|e)?cx|c[lh])/)
					clear_provenance("rcx")
				if (opcode ~ /wrmsr/) {
					if (!("rcx" in value))
						print "unknown"
					else
						print value["rcx"]
					clear_provenance("")
				}
			}

		function canonical(register_name, name) {
			name = register_name
			gsub(/^%/, "", name)
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(ax|al|ah)$/) return "rax"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(bx|bl|bh)$/) return "rbx"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(cx|cl|ch)$/) return "rcx"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(dx|dl|dh)$/) return "rdx"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(si|sil)$/) return "rsi"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(di|dil)$/) return "rdi"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(bp|bpl)$/) return "rbp"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(sp|spl)$/) return "rsp"
			if (name ~ /^r(8|9|1[0-5])(d|w|b)?$/) {
				sub(/[dwb]$/, "", name)
				return name
			}
			return ""
		}
		function width(register_name, name) {
			name = register_name
			gsub(/^%/, "", name)
			if (name ~ /^r(ax|bx|cx|dx|si|di|bp|sp)$/ ||
			    name ~ /^r(8|9|1[0-5])$/)
				return 64
			if (name ~ /^e(ax|bx|cx|dx|si|di|bp|sp)$/ ||
			    name ~ /^r(8|9|1[0-5])d$/)
				return 32
			if (name ~ /^(ax|bx|cx|dx|si|di|bp|sp)$/ ||
			    name ~ /^r(8|9|1[0-5])w$/)
				return 16
			return 8
		}
		function clear_provenance(register_name) {
			if (register_name == "") {
				for (name in value)
					delete value[name]
			} else {
				delete value[register_name]
			}
		}
		function is_rdpru(instruction) {
			while (instruction ~ /^(addr16|addr32|cs|ds|es|fs|gs|ss)/ ||
			       instruction ~ /^rex(\.[WRXB]+)?/) {
				sub(/^(addr16|addr32|cs|ds|es|fs|gs|ss)/, "", instruction)
				sub(/^rex(\.[WRXB]+)?/, "", instruction)
			}
			return instruction == "rdpru"
		}
		function clear_implicit_destinations(instruction, operands) {
			operands = instruction
			gsub(/^[a-z0-9]+/, "", operands)
			if (instruction ~ /^(mul|div|idiv)[a-z]*%/ ||
			    (instruction ~ /^imul[a-z]*%/ && operands !~ /,/)) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(cbw|cwde|cdqe)$/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(cwd|cdq|cqo)$/) {
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(cpuid|getsec)$/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rbx")
				clear_provenance("rcx")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(rdmsr|rdtsc|xgetbv)$/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(rdpmc|rdpkru)$/ || is_rdpru(instruction)) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^rdtscp$/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rcx")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^cmpxchg(8b|16b)/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^cmpxchg/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(lahf|xlat)/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(lods)/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rsi")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(movs|cmps)/) {
				clear_provenance("rsi")
				clear_provenance("rdi")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(stos|scas|ins)/) {
				clear_provenance("rdi")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^outs/) {
				clear_provenance("rsi")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(enter|leave|push|pop)/) {
				clear_provenance("rsp")
				if (instruction ~ /^(enter|leave)/)
					clear_provenance("rbp")
			}
			if (instruction ~ /^(loop|loope|loopne)/)
				clear_provenance("rcx")
			if (instruction ~ /^(rsm|syscall|sysret|rep)/)
				clear_provenance("")
		}
		function normalize(address) {
			sub(/^0x/, "", address)
			sub(/^0+/, "", address)
			return address == "" ? "0" : address
		}
		{
			line[NR] = $0
			if ($0 ~ /\t((lock|bnd)[[:space:]]+)?(j[a-z]+|loop[a-z]*)[[:space:]]+(0x)?[0-9a-f]+/) {
				destination = $0
				sub(/^.*\t((lock|bnd)[[:space:]]+)?(j[a-z]+|loop[a-z]*)[[:space:]]+/, "", destination)
				sub(/[[:space:]].*$/, "", destination)
				branch_target[normalize(destination)] = 1
			}
		}
		END {
			for (number = 1; number <= NR; number++) {
				text = line[number]
				if (text ~ /^Disassembly of section /) {
					clear_provenance("")
					continue
				}
				if (text ~ /^[0-9a-f]+ <[^>]+>:/) {
					clear_provenance("")
					continue
				}
				if (text !~ /^[[:space:]]*[0-9a-f]+:/)

				address = text

				address = normalize(address)

					clear_provenance("")
				instruction = text
				sub(/^.*\t/, "", instruction)
				gsub(/[[:space:]]+/, "", instruction)
				opcode = instruction
				sub(/^(lock|bnd)/, "", opcode)
				if (opcode ~ /^(j[a-z]+|loop[a-z]*|call|ret)/) {
					clear_provenance("")
					continue
				}
				if (opcode ~ /^mov[a-z]*\$0x[0-9a-f]+,%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					source = opcode
					sub(/^mov[a-z]*\$0x/, "", source)
					sub(/,.*/, "", source)
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					destination_width = width(destination)
					destination = canonical(destination)
					if (destination != "") {
						if (destination_width >= 32)
							value[destination] = source
						else
							value[destination] = "dynamic"
					}
				} else if (opcode ~ /^mov[a-z]*%[a-z0-9]+,%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					source = opcode
					sub(/^mov[a-z]*/, "", source)
					sub(/,.*/, "", source)
					source_width = width(source)
					source = canonical(source)
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					destination_width = width(destination)
					destination = canonical(destination)

						if (destination_width >= 32 && source_width >= 32 &&

							value[destination] = value[source]
						else
							value[destination] = "dynamic"
					}

					sub(/,.*/, "", source)
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)

					destination = opcode

					sub(/,.*/, "", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))

					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))

					sub(/^[^%]*/, "%", destination)

		function is_rdpru(instruction) {
			while (instruction ~ /^(addr16|addr32|cs|ds|es|fs|gs|ss)/ ||
			       instruction ~ /^rex(\.[WRXB]+)?/) {
				sub(/^(addr16|addr32|cs|ds|es|fs|gs|ss)/, "", instruction)
				sub(/^rex(\.[WRXB]+)?/, "", instruction)
			}
			return instruction == "rdpru"
		}
		function clear_implicit_destinations(instruction, operands) {
			operands = instruction
			gsub(/^[a-z0-9]+/, "", operands)
			if (instruction ~ /^(mul|div|idiv)[a-z]*%/ ||
			    (instruction ~ /^imul[a-z]*%/ && operands !~ /,/)) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(cbw|cwde|cdqe)$/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(cwd|cdq|cqo)$/) {
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(cpuid|getsec)$/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rbx")
				clear_provenance("rcx")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(rdmsr|rdtsc|xgetbv)$/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(rdpmc|rdpkru)$/ || is_rdpru(instruction)) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^rdtscp$/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rcx")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^cmpxchg(8b|16b)/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rdx")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^cmpxchg/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(lahf|xlat)/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(lods)/) {
				clear_provenance("rax")
				clear_provenance("rsi")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(movs|cmps)/) {
				clear_provenance("rsi")
				clear_provenance("rdi")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(stos|scas|ins)/) {
				clear_provenance("rdi")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^outs/) {
				clear_provenance("rsi")
			} else if (instruction ~ /^(enter|leave|push|pop)/) {
				clear_provenance("rsp")
				if (instruction ~ /^(enter|leave)/)
					clear_provenance("rbp")
			}
			if (instruction ~ /^(loop|loope|loopne)/)
				clear_provenance("rcx")
			if (instruction ~ /^(rsm|syscall|sysret|rep)/)
				clear_provenance("")
		}
		function normalize(address) {
			sub(/^0x/, "", address)
			sub(/^0+/, "", address)
			return address == "" ? "0" : address
		}
		{
			line[NR] = $0
			if ($0 ~ /\t((lock|bnd)[[:space:]]+)?(j[a-z]+|loop[a-z]*)[[:space:]]+(0x)?[0-9a-f]+/) {
				destination = $0
				sub(/^.*\t((lock|bnd)[[:space:]]+)?(j[a-z]+|loop[a-z]*)[[:space:]]+/, "", destination)
				sub(/[[:space:]].*$/, "", destination)
				branch_target[normalize(destination)] = 1
			}
		}
		END {
			for (number = 1; number <= NR; number++) {
				text = line[number]
				if (text ~ /^Disassembly of section /) {
					clear_provenance("")
					continue
				}
				if (text ~ /^[0-9a-f]+ <[^>]+>:/) {
					clear_provenance("")
					continue
				}
				if (text !~ /^[[:space:]]*[0-9a-f]+:/)
					continue
				address = text
				sub(/^[[:space:]]*/, "", address)
				sub(/:.*/, "", address)
				address = normalize(address)
				if (address in branch_target)
					clear_provenance("")
				instruction = text
				sub(/^.*\t/, "", instruction)
				gsub(/[[:space:]]+/, "", instruction)
				opcode = instruction
				sub(/^(lock|bnd)/, "", opcode)
				if (opcode ~ /^(j[a-z]+|loop[a-z]*|call|ret)/) {
					clear_provenance("")
					continue
				}
				if (opcode ~ /^mov[a-z]*\$0x[0-9a-f]+,%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					source = opcode
					sub(/^mov[a-z]*\$0x/, "", source)
					sub(/,.*/, "", source)
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					destination_width = width(destination)
					destination = canonical(destination)
					if (destination != "") {
						if (destination_width >= 32)
							value[destination] = source
						else
							value[destination] = "dynamic"
					}
				} else if (opcode ~ /^mov[a-z]*%[a-z0-9]+,%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					source = opcode
					sub(/^mov[a-z]*/, "", source)
					sub(/,.*/, "", source)
					source_width = width(source)
					source = canonical(source)
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					destination_width = width(destination)
					destination = canonical(destination)
					if (destination != "") {
						if (destination_width >= 32 && source_width >= 32 &&
						    source in value)
							value[destination] = value[source]
						else
							value[destination] = "dynamic"
					}
				} else if (opcode ~ /^(xchg|xadd)/) {
					source = opcode
					sub(/^[^%]*/, "%", source)
					sub(/,.*/, "", source)
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(source))
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
				} else if (opcode ~ /^mulx[a-z]*%[a-z0-9]+,%[a-z0-9]+,%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^[^,]*,/, "", destination)
					sub(/,.*/, "", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
				} else if (opcode ~ /^(rdfsbase|rdgsbase|rdpid|rdsspq)[a-z]*%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^[^%]*/, "%", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
				} else if (opcode ~ /^(pop|inc|dec|neg|not|bswap|set[a-z]+|rol|ror|rcl|rcr|sal|sar|shl|shr|rdrand|rdseed)[a-z]*%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^[^%]*/, "%", destination)
					clear_provenance(canonical(destination))
				} else if (opcode ~ /,%[a-z0-9]+$/) {
					destination = opcode
					sub(/^.*,%/, "%", destination)
					destination = canonical(destination)
					clear_provenance(destination)
				}
				clear_implicit_destinations(opcode)
				if (opcode !~ /^mov/ && opcode !~ /^(cpuid|getsec|rdtscp)/ &&
				     opcode ~ /%((r|e)?cx|c[lh])/)
					clear_provenance("rcx")
				if (opcode ~ /wrmsr/) {
					if (!("rcx" in value))
						print "unknown"
					else
						print value["rcx"]
					clear_provenance("")
				}
			}

		function canonical(register_name, name) {
			name = register_name
			gsub(/^%/, "", name)
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(ax|al|ah)$/) return "rax"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(bx|bl|bh)$/) return "rbx"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(cx|cl|ch)$/) return "rcx"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(dx|dl|dh)$/) return "rdx"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(si|sil)$/) return "rsi"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(di|dil)$/) return "rdi"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(bp|bpl)$/) return "rbp"
			if (name ~ /^(r|e)?(sp|spl)$/) return "rsp"
			if (name ~ /^r(8|9|1[0-5])(d|w|b)?$/) {
				sub(/[dwb]$/, "", name)
				return name
			}
			return ""
		}
		function width(register_name, name) {
			name = register_name
			gsub(/^%/, "", name)
			if (name ~ /^r(ax|bx|cx|dx|si|di|bp|sp)$/ ||
			    name ~ /^r(8|9|1[0-5])$/)
				return 64
			if (name ~ /^e(ax|bx|cx|dx|si|di|bp|sp)$/ ||
			    name ~ /^r(8|9|1[0-5])d$/)
				return 32
			if (name ~ /^(ax|bx|cx|dx|si|di|bp|sp)$/ ||
			    name ~ /^r(8|9|1[0-5])w$/)
				return 16
			return 8
		}
		function clear_provenance(register_name) {
			if (register_name == "") {
				for (name in value)
					delete value[name]
			} else {
				delete value[register_name]
			}
		}

		}
